More precisely p_i = r_{i mod q}/n? Sum r over q =1? Let r_j≥0, ∑r=1. p_i = r_{i mod q}/n. Then for large n, h_k ≈ (n/q) * s_j / n^2? Where s_j = ∑ r_i r_{k-i mod q} (circular conv). Wait number of i for each residue a is n/q, for b determined. So for each a, count n/q, sum over a q => total q*(n/q)*(r_a r_b/n^2)= (1/n)∑_a r_a r_b. So h_j≈s_j/n where s_j is circular convolution of r with itself (∑_a r_a r_{a+j}). Yes no 1/q. Because p sum=1, expected sum of two iid periodic residues: h_j≈1/n * P(R+R≡j) where R has distribution r. So h_j = s_j/n. Objective V=2n*h_max=2 max_j s_j. Thus for large n, the score of a periodic pattern equals twice the maximum coefficient of the circular self-convolution of a probability distribution r on a q-cycle. Boundary effects O(1/n). This is huge: we can choose any period q and optimize r over simplex length q; asymptotic score = 2 * max_{j≠? j=0?} circular conv? j includes 0. But for circular conv, average=1/q. Could design a deterministic algorithm based on projected gradient with smoothing/annealing:
1. Choose n (maybe 32768 or 49152). Initialize p from several profiles: uniform, linear ramp, two-block optimized, random, parent resampled.

```
    n=len(p_init)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(p_init,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
    p=_softmax(z)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_sc=_score(p)
    best_shape maybe.
    m=zeros; v=zeros; t=0
    for beta in betas:
        # maybe reset moments? If reset to zero each beta, less momentum from previous; maybe keep.
        # For each beta, max iterations based on time per iter. We'll loop while time.
        iter_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic()+ segment_time?) We don't know segment. Use deadline and betas; if time left <5 break.
        for it in range(max_iters_beta):

            h=_convolve_self(p); hmax=h.max()
```

```
Let's implement ADAM LSE as main global optimizer:
```
def _adam_lse(p_init, deadline, warm_lr=0.1, betas=None, min_time=5):
   z=log(p); p=softmax(z)
   best= score
   m=v=t=0
   # adaptive lr? start 0.2
   for beta in betas:
       # maybe reset t? Keep moments
       while time < deadline:
           h=conv(p); hmax=h.max(); sc=2n hmax
           update best
           w=softmax((h-hmax)/beta)
           g=n*conv_weighted(p,w)
           gz=p*(g-g.mean())
           # ADAM
           t+=1
           m=...; v=...
           step=mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
           z_new=z - lr*step
           # maybe limit step max 0.2?
           step_norm=np.linalg.norm(step)
           if step_norm>1: step=step/step_norm  # ADAM step per coord around lr but can huge early
           p_new=softmax(z-lr*step)
           # optional if p_new has sum?; score if time?
           z=z_new; p=p_new
       # after beta, maybe reset moments? no
   return best
```
ADAM step can be ~1 per coordinate, z changes 1, p major. lr=0.05 safe. Use `ams`? no.

Let's implement `_adam_lse(p_init, deadline, min_time=5, lr=0.03, b1=0.9, b2=0.999, betas=None)`:
- `h_inst = _convolve_self(p); hmax = h.max(); if hmax==0 return`
- If betas None: `scale = max(1.0/n, 1e-8); betas = np.array([1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002,0.0001,0.00005,0.00002,0.00001]) * scale * 0.5?` Starting beta maybe 0.5/n=1.5e-5. If current hmax=3e-5, beta smaller than hmax. Good.
- For each beta:
   - `max_iter = max(50, int((deadline - now)/est_iter))` but we don't want all beta equal. Use while time and an iteration cap maybe 10000 total. We can loop over beta until deadline. For each beta, do e.g. `iters = max(100, min(5000, int(estimated_time_left / (len(remaining_betas)*est_iter))))`? Simpler: for each beta, run until time consumed proportionally: `seg_deadline = min(deadline, start+segment_fraction*budget)`. But function only gets deadline, not start. We can allocate based on global? We'll pass `time_limit` absolute.
Simpler: define `max_total_iter=20000`. For beta index b, `iters = max(20, max_total_iter // (len(betas)-b))` but time varies. Use while time<deadline and iter<=max_iter.
- In loop, compute h, weights, grad, ADAM. Track best score. If current score < best_sc -1e-12: best. If current score > best_sc*1.05 and stagnation count >50, reset z to best log, reset moments? We'll implement.
- To avoid evaluating score extra, use hmax from current. For best update, sc=2n hmax.
- ADAM lr can adapt: Start `lr=0.05`. If no improvement for 100 iters, lr*=0.8. If improvement, maybe lr=min(0.1, lr*1.01).
- Step clipping: `step = mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps)`, norm maybe >10; clip to [-2,2] per coord? Use `np.clip(step, -2, 2)`. This prevents huge z changes early.
- After z update, p=softmax. Could enforce floor? softmax positive.

Potential issue: Minimizing LSE with β too large from parent may move away. We can skip high beta if p already good? Use beta starting at `0.1 * hmax` (≈3e-6) and anneal to 1e-9. This polishes without large moves. But to escape local minima, high β needed. We'll run separate ILS for escapes.

ILS:
- `_ils(p_best, deadline, rng, min_time=60)`: maintains current=best. Repeat until time:
   - Perturb current with multiplicative noise in log space with sigma chosen. Strategies:
      * global Gaussian sigma 0.05,0.2,0.5 in log (z change). Since p entries O1/n, log noise O1 changes relative large.
      * block: choose random block length n/16, perturb within.
      * sparse: perturb subset.
      * flip blocks? reverse.
   - For each perturbed, run quick optimize: `_adam_lse` with small time (e.g., 3s) and maybe active polish 0.5s. Use `deadline_sub = min(deadline-min_time, time+5)`.
   - If improved best, accept and set current; else keep if better than current? Could use simulated annealing accept if score < current*1.02? But we need best; simple hill climbing.
- Quick optimize should be cheap. `_quick_optimize(p, deadline_quick)`: run ADAM with low betas [0.01,0.001,0.0001]/n? or hard active. We'll implement.
```

def _project_simplex(v):
    n=len(v); u=np.sort(v)[::-1]; cssv=np.cumsum(u)-1; ind=np.where(u - cssv/n >0)[0]; max_ind=ind[-1]; theta=cssv[max_ind]/(max_ind+1); return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```

import numpy as np
import time

PROPOSE_START = 0.0

def _next_pow2(m): ...
def _convolve_self(p): ...
def _grad_h(p,w): ...
def _score(p): ...
def _normalize(p): ...
def _softmax_weights(h,beta): ...
def _active_weights(h, tol_abs): ...
def _resample(p, new_n): ...

def _adam_lse(p_init, deadline, lr=0.03, b1=0.9,b2=0.999, clip=0.5, eta=0.0, min_time=3.0):
    # eta maybe start time? not needed
    p=_normalize(p_init.astype np.float64)
    n=len(p)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
    p=_softmax(z)
    best=p.copy(); best_sc=_score(p)
    h=_convolve_self(p); hmax=float(h.max())
    if hmax <=0: return best,best_sc
    scale=1.0/n
    betas = np.array([...]) * scale
    # if current hmax smaller, maybe multiply betas by hmax*scale? Use fractions of hmax: betas = hmax * fractions
    fractions = [0.5,0.3,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.03,0.015,0.008,0.004,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002,0.0001,0.00005,0.00002]
    # but if hmax changes, dynamic beta = base_frac*hmax? We'll fixed initial hmax*frac, min 1e-8 scale? 
    betas = hmax * np.array(fractions)
    betas = np.maximum(betas, 1e-6*scale) # maybe
    m=zeros; v=zeros; t=0; lr_cur=lr; no_impr=0
    for beta in betas:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - min_time: break
       # limit iterations per beta maybe max 4000
       max_iter_beta = 4000
       for _ in range(max_iter_beta):
          now=time.monotonic(); if now>deadline-min_time: break
          h=_convolve_self(p); hmax=float(h.max()); sc=2.0*n*hmax
          if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
             best=p.copy(); best_sc=sc; no_impr=0
          else: no_impr+=1
          if no_impr > 200:
             # reset to best and reduce lr
             p=best.copy(); z=np.log(...); m=v=0; lr_cur*=0.7; no_impr=0
          w=_lse_weights(h,beta)
          g=n*_grad_h(p,w)
          gz=p*(g - np.mean(g))
          t+=1; m=...; v=...
          step=mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
          step=np.clip(step, -clip, clip)
          z_new=z - lr_cur*step
          p_new=_softmax(z_new)
          # if p_new has issues? 
          if np.any(~np.isfinite(p_new)): p_new=best.copy(); z=...
          z=z_new; p=p_new
    # final few small beta active? Could call active here outside.
    return best,best_sc
```

`_active_polish`:
```
def _active_polish(p_init, deadline, tol_frac_start=0.2, tol_frac_end=1e-5, lr_init=0.2, momentum=0.85, min_time=2.0):
   p=normalize; best=...; v=zeros; tol_frac=...; lr=lr_init; no_imp=0; max_stall=400
   while time<deadline-min_time:
      h=conv; hmax; sc=2n hmax
      if sc<best: update no_imp=0
      else no_imp+=1
      tol_abs = max(tol_frac*hmax, 1e-12)
      w=active_weights(h,tol_abs)
      grad=_grad_h(p,w)
      # scale grad? We'll use grad as is. lr maybe 0.1. If grad norm =0 break.
      v = momentum*v - lr*grad
      alpha=1.0; improved=False; current_sc=sc
      for _ in range(30):
         p_try=_normalize(p + alpha*v)
         sc_try=2*n*float(_convolve_self(p_try).max())
         if sc_try < current_sc - 1e-15:
             p=p_try; improved=True; break
         alpha *=0.5
      if not improved:
         v=zeros; no_imp+=1
         if no_imp%20==0:
             tol_frac=max(tol_frac_end, tol_frac*0.6)
             lr*=0.7
         if no_imp>max_stall:
             p=best.copy(); v=zeros; tol_frac=max(tol_frac_end, tol_frac*0.4); lr=lr_init*0.5; no_imp=0
      else:
         if tol_frac>tol_frac_end: tol_frac=max(tol_frac_end, tol_frac*0.98)
         lr=min(lr_init*2, lr*1.005)
   return best, score(best)
```

Maybe use a fixed small step without line search? Since v may be stable. But line search ensures descent.

`_ils`:
```
def _ils(p_best, deadline, rng, min_time=60, max_iter=None):
   current=normalize(p_best); current_sc=score(current); best=current.copy(); best_sc=current_sc
   strategies = [(0.1,'global'),(0.3,'global'),(0.6,'global'),(0.2,'block'),(0.4,'sparse'),(0.2,'mirror'),(0.1,'global')]
   attempt=0
   while time<deadline-min_time:
      kind,sigma=strategies[attempt%len]
      p_pert=perturb(current,kind,sigma,n,rng)
      opt_deadline=min(deadline-min_time, time+3.0 + (0.5 if improved_near else 0))
      p_opt=sc_quick(p_pert,opt_deadline) # active polish 2-4s
      sc_opt=score(p_opt)
      if sc_opt < best_sc - 1e-12: best=p_opt; current=p_opt; best_sc=sc; print
      elif sc_opt < current_sc: current=p_opt; current_sc=sc
      attempt++
   return best,best_sc
```
Perturb functions top-level `_perturb_p(p, kind, sigma, rng)`.
- global: z=log(p)+sigma normal; softmax.
- block: choose len max(64,n//16), random start, perturb that block only in log; normalize.
- sparse: choose subset n//8, multiply by exp(sigma normal).
- mirror: reverse a segment, add noise.
Use p positive; log floor.

Detailed function implementations:

```
def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(2, x))
    k=2
    while k < x: k <<= 1
    return k
```

`_convolve_self(p)`:
```
n = len(p)
out = 2*n - 1
N = _next_pow2(out)
f = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
y = np.fft.irfft(f*f, N)
y = y[:out]
# clip tiny negative
y = np.clip(y, 0.0, None)
return y
```
For large p, f*f may have small imaginary? irfft returns real. Good.

`_grad_h(p,w)`:
```
n=len(p); L=len(w)
out = n + L - 1
N = _next_pow2(out)
fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
pr = p[::-1]
fp = np.fft.rfft(pr, N)
y = np.fft.irfft(fw*fp, N)
return 2.0 * y[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Could `w` have length not 2n-1? We'll ensure.

`_score(p)`:
```
p=np.asarray(p,float)
s=p.sum()
if s < 1e-30: return np.inf
p=p/s
n=len(p)
h=_convolve_self(p)
return float(2.0*n*h.max())
```
But often p already normalized; this recomputes sum. For speed inside optimizers, use `_score_norm` assuming sum1:
```
def _score_norm(p): return 2.0*len(p)*float(_convolve_self(p).max())
```
Use `_score` for final/initial.

`_normalize(p)`:
```
p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
p=np.clip(p,0.0,None)
s=p.sum()
if not np.isfinite(s) or s<1e-30:
   return np.ones_like(p)/len(p)
return p/s
```
If p length 0, return [1.0]? handle.

`_softmax(z)`:
```
z=np.asarray(z,float)
z=z-np.max(z)
e=np.exp(np.clip(z,-745,700))
s=e.sum()
if s<1e-300: return np.ones_like(z)/len(z)
return e/s
```

`_log_p(p)`:
```
z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
return z - z.mean()
```

`_lse_weights(h,beta)`:
```
hmax=float(h.max())
if beta <=0 or not finite: return onehot
x=(h-hmax)/beta
x=np.clip(x,-745,0.0)
e=np.exp(x); s=e.sum()
if s<1e-300: onehot
return e/s
```

`_onehot(h)`:
```
w=np.zeros_like(h); w[int(np.argmax(h))]=1; return w
```

`_active_weights(h, tol_abs)`:
```
hmax=float(h.max())
if tol_abs <0: tol_abs=0
active = h >= (hmax - tol_abs)
if not active.any(): active[argmax]=True
gap = hmax - h
eps = max(1e-15*hmax, 1e-300)
w=np.zeros_like(h)
w[active] = 1.0 / (gap[active] + eps)
s=w.sum(); if s<...; return w/s
```

`_resample(p,new_n)`:
```
old=p; if len=0 return ones/new
x_old=np.linspace(0,1,len(old))
x_new=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,old)
return _normalize(new)
```

Perturb:
```
def _perturb_global(p,sigma,rng):
   z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); z = z + sigma*rng.standard_normal(len(p)); return _softmax(z)
```
`_perturb_block(p,sigma,rng)`:
```
n=len(p); blen=max(16,n//16); start=rng.integers(0,n-blen)
z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
z[start:start+blen]+=sigma*rng.standard_normal(blen)
return _softmax(z)
```
`_perturb_sparse`: idx=rng.choice(n,n//8,False); factors=np.exp(sigma*rng.standard_normal(len(idx))); p2=p.copy(); p2[idx]*=factors; return normalize.
`_perturb_mirror`: seg_len=max(16,n//16); start; p2 copy; p2[start:start+seg_len]=p2[...] [::-1]; add small global noise 0.02; return.

Block expand:
```
def _expand_q(q,B,l):
   q=_normalize(q)
   val=q/float(l)
   return np.repeat(val, l)
```
If n not exactly B*l, choose B divisor. We'll require.

`_q_gradient(gp,B,l)`:
```
return l * gp.reshape(B,l).sum(axis=1)
```

Block ADAM:
```
def _adam_q(q_init, n, B, deadline, lr=0.05, min_time=5.0):
    l=n//B
    q=_normalize(q_init)
    z=_log_p(q)
    best_q=q.copy(); best_p=_expand_q(q,B,l); best_sc=_score_norm(best_p)
    m=np.zeros(B); v=np.zeros(B); t=0; lr_cur=lr; no_impr=0
    # betas fractions of current hmax? Initialize p, h, hmax
    h=_convolve_self(best_p); hmax=h.max(); if hmax==0...
    betas = max(hmax,1.0/n) * np.array([...fracs...])
    if time < deadline:
       for beta in betas:
          max_iter=10000
          for _ in range(max_iter):
             if now>deadline-min_time: break
             q=_softmax(z); p=_expand_q(q,B,l)
             h=_convolve_self(p); hmax=...; sc=2n hmax
             if sc<best_sc: update best_q,best_p
             else no_impr++
             if no_impr>300 and sc>best_sc*1.02: reset z to best_q, m=v=0, lr*=0.7, no_impr=0
             w=_lse_weights(h,beta)
             gp=_grad_h(p,w)
             gq=_q_gradient(gp,B,l)
             gz=q*(gq - gq.mean())
             t+=1; m=...; v=...
             step=mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+1e-8); step=clip(step,-1,1)
             z = z - lr_cur*step
    return best_p,best_sc
```
Since q dimension small, reset threshold lower.

Could also run active_q with weights active and line search, but ADAM enough.

```
def _block_starts(parent_n, B):

   # linear: z=np.linspace(-1,1,B); q=softmax(0.5z)
```

Full ADAM:
We could implement `_adam_full` similarly to `_adam_q` but p dimension n. It might be redundant with active polish. Let's implement `_adam_full` as global smoother. Use LSE weights. It can improve block p. We'll include.

```
def _adam_full(p_init, deadline, lr=0.02, min_time=5):
   ... similar to _adam_q but n dimension, z log p, betas hmax fractions. Use clip step 0.5.
```

Why both full and active? Full ADAM may handle smooth LSE better. Active may shave. We'll call both.

Time management in propose:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    global PROPOSE_START
    start=time.monotonic(); PROPOSE_START=start; deadline=start+max(1,float(budget_s)-20)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best=None; best_sc=np.inf
    # load parent
    parent=None
    try:
       arr=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if arr.size>0:
          parent=_normalize(arr)
    except NameError: pass
    n_main=24576
    if parent is not None:
       if len(parent)==n_main: best=parent.copy()
       else: best=_resample(parent,n_main)
    else: best=_normalize(np.ones(n_main))
    best_sc=_score_norm(best)
    print final
    # Phase 1: active polish parent
    if time<deadline-30:
       p1,sc1=_active_polish(best, min(deadline, start+250), tol_start=0.2, tol_end=1e-5, lr=0.2)
       if sc1<best_sc: update
    # Phase 2: full adam from best
    if time<deadline-60:
       p2,sc2=_adam_full(best, min(deadline, start+150) maybe use absolute end = min(deadline, time+150), lr=0.03)
       update
    # Phase 3: block optimization
    if time<deadline-120:
       block_deadline=min(deadline, time+250)
       # choose B=256 maybe
       p_b,sc_b=_run_block_search(best, n_main, 256, block_deadline, rng)
       update
    # Phase 4: active polish best
    if time<deadline-200:
       p4,sc4=_active_polish(best, min(deadline, time+250), tol_start=0.1,tol_end=1e-6,lr=0.15)
       update
    # Phase 5: ILS
    if time<deadline-120:
       p5,sc5=_ils(best, min(deadline, time+350), rng, min_time=80)
       update
    # Phase 6: try n2=32768
    n2=32768
    if time<deadline-150:
       p_res=_resample(best,n2)
       p6,sc6=_active_polish(p_res, min(deadline, time+250), tol_start=0.15,tol_end=1e-6,lr=0.15)
       if sc6<best_sc: update
    # Phase 7: final active polish
    if time<deadline-5:
       p7,sc7=_active_polish(best, deadline-2, tol_start=0.05,tol_end=1e-7,lr=0.1)
       update
    final = np.clip(best,0,None); ensure sum/length; return list floats
```
Need ensure deadlines compute absolute times, not `start+` after previous. Use `phase_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + duration)`. For parent polish after no previous, `min(deadline, start+250)`. For subsequent, `time.monotonic()+duration`. Good.

Total durations: 250+150+250+250+350+250+final ~1700 + overhead, okay. But if previous phases skipped, deadlines leave more. We can be more adaptive: allocate proportional but ensure final.

Maybe block search 250s plus full adam 150; if parent already good, parent polish 250 may be enough. We need not overdo. Use time checks.

`_run_block_search`:
```
def _run_block_search(best_p, n, B, deadline, rng):
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2 * n * max(np.convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a)**2)

The code combines:
- a strong active-peak gradient polish in the simplex,
- a smoothed log-sum-exp ADAM optimizer in log-weights,
- a reduced block-continuous optimizer to find new basins,
- stochastic iterated local search with quick polishing,
- and a final multi-length exploration.

All routines are top-level and avoid closures/lambdas.
"""

import time
import numpy as np


PROPOSE_START = 0.0


def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(2, x))
    k = 2
    while k < x:
        k <<= 1
    return k


def _convolve_self(p):
    n = len(p)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    out_n = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(out_n)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    y = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, N)
    y = y[:out_n]
    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, None)
    return y


def _grad_h(p, w):
    """
    Gradient of the weighted convolution sum sum_k w[k] * (p*p)[k]
    with respect to p.  Implicit factor is 2.
    """
    n = len(p)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = len(w)
    out_n = n + L - 1
    N = _next_pow2(out_n)

    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    pr = p[::-1]
    fp = np.fft.rfft(pr, N)
    y = np.fft.irfft(fw * fp, N)
    return 2.0 * y[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def _score_norm(p):
    """Score assuming sum(p) is 1 and p is non-negative."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = len(p)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-10:
        p = p / s
    h = _convolve_self(p)
    if h.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * float(h.max()))


def _normalize(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones_like(p, dtype=np.float64) / float(p.size)
    return p / s


def _log_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    if z.size:
        z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    return z


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -745.0, 700.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones_like(z, dtype=np.float64) / float(z.size)
    return e / s


def _onehot_weights(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if h.size == 0:
        return np.ones(0, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.zeros_like(h, dtype=np.float64)
    w[int(np.argmax(h))] = 1.0
    return w


def _lse_weights(h, beta):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if h.size == 0:
        return w
    hmax = float(h.max())
    if hmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(hmax):
        return _onehot_weights(h)
    if beta <= 1e-300:
        beta = 1e-300
    x = (h - hmax) / beta
    x = np.clip(x, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return _onehot_weights(h)
    return e / s


def _active_weights(h, tol_abs):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if h.size == 0:
        return w
    hmax = float(h.max())
    if hmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(hmax):
        return _onehot_weights(h)
    if tol_abs < 0.0:
        tol_abs = 0.0
    active = (h >= (hmax - tol_abs))
    if not np.any(active):
        active = np.zeros_like(h, dtype=bool)
        active[int(np.argmax(h))] = True

    gap = hmax - h
    eps = max(1e-15 * hmax, 1e-300)
    w = np.zeros_like(h, dtype=np.float64)
    w[active] = 1.0 / (gap[active] + eps)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return _onehot_weights(h)
    return w / s


def _resample(p, new_n):
    p = _normalize(p)
    old_n = len(p)
    if old_n == int(new_n):
        return p.copy()
    if old_n <= 1 or new_n <= 0:
        return np.ones(int(max(1, new_n)), dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(new_n), dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    return _normalize(y)


def _adam_full(p_init, deadline, lr=0.02, b1=0.90, b2=0.999,
               step_clip=0.30, min_time=3.0):
    """
    Smoothed log-sum-exp ADAM in log-softmax coordinates.
    """
    p = _normalize(p_init)
    n = len(p)
    if n <= 0:
        return p, np.inf
    z = _log_p(p)
    p = _softmax(z)
    best = p.copy()
    best_sc = _score_norm(best)

    h = _convolve_self(best)
    hmax = float(h.max())
    if hmax <= 0.0:
        return best, best_sc

    beta_fracs = np.array([
        0.50, 0.30, 0.20, 0.12, 0.07, 0.04, 0.02, 0.012,
        0.007, 0.004, 0.002, 0.0012, 0.0007, 0.0004,
        0.0002, 0.00010, 0.00006, 0.00003, 0.000015
    ], dtype=np.float64)
    min_beta = 1e-12

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    lr_cur = float(lr)
    no_improve = 0

    hard_drop = 0.0
    for frac in beta_fracs:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - max(1.0, min_time):
            break
        for _ in range(200000):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now > deadline - max(1.0, min_time):
                break

            beta = max(min_beta, float(frac) * hmax)
            h = _convolve_self(p)
            hmax = float(h.max())
            sc = 2.0 * n * hmax

            if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc <= 0.0:
                break

            if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best = p.copy()
                best_sc = float(sc)
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1

            if no_improve > 600:
                if sc > best_sc * (1.0 + 0.005 + hard_drop):
                    p = best.copy()
                    z = _log_p(p)
                    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    t = 0
                    lr_cur *= 0.75
                    hard_drop = max(0.0, hard_drop - 0.002)
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    lr_cur = max(0.002, lr_cur * 0.92)
                    no_improve = 0

            w = _lse_weights(h, beta)
            gp = _grad_h(p, w)
            gp = np.where(np.isfinite(gp), gp, 0.0)
            g = float(n) * gp
            gz = p * (g - np.mean(g))
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(gz)):
                break

            t += 1
            bias1 = 1.0 - b1 ** t
            bias2 = 1.0 - b2 ** t
            m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * gz
            v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (gz * gz)
            mhat = m / bias1
            vhat = v / bias2
            step = mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-8)
            step = np.clip(step, -float(step_clip), float(step_clip))
            z_new = z - float(lr_cur) * step
            z_new = np.where(np.isfinite(z_new), z_new, 0.0)

            p_new = _softmax(z_new)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(p_new)):
                p_new = best.copy()
                z_new = _log_p(p_new)

            z = z_new
            p = p_new

    return best, best_sc


def _adam_block(q_init, n, B, deadline, lr=0.08, b1=0.90, b2=0.999,
                step_clip=1.0, min_time=3.0):
    """
    Optimize a block-constant profile q (length B), then return the expanded
    full-length sequence.
    """
    B = int(B)
    n = int(n)
    if B <= 0 or n <= 0 or n % B != 0:
        q = _normalize(np.ones(B, dtype=np.float64))
        l = max(1, n // B)
        return _normalize(np.repeat(q / float(l), l)), _score_norm(_normalize(np.repeat(q / float(l), l)))

    l = n // B
    q = _normalize(q_init)
    z = _log_p(q)
    q = _softmax(z)
    p = _normalize(np.repeat(q / float(l), l))
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = _score_norm(best_p)

    h = _convolve_self(best_p)
    hmax = float(h.max())
    if hmax <= 0.0:
        return best_p, best_sc

    beta_fracs = np.array([
        0.50, 0.30, 0.20, 0.12, 0.07, 0.04, 0.02, 0.012,
        0.007, 0.004, 0.002, 0.0012, 0.0007, 0.0004,
        0.0002, 0.00010, 0.00006, 0.00003, 0.000015
    ], dtype=np.float64)
    min_beta = 1e-12

    m = np.zeros(B, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(B, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    lr_cur = float(lr)
    no_improve = 0
    hard_drop = 0.0

    for frac in beta_fracs:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - max(1.0, min_time):
            break
        for _ in range(200000):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now > deadline - max(1.0, min_time):
                break

            beta = max(min_beta, float(frac) * hmax)
            q = _softmax(z)
            p = _normalize(np.repeat(q / float(l), l))

            h = _convolve_self(p)
            hmax = float(h.max())
            sc = 2.0 * n * hmax
            if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc <= 0.0:
                break

            if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best_p = p.copy()
                best_sc = float(sc)
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1

            if no_improve > 400:
                if sc > best_sc * (1.0 + 0.004 + hard_drop):
                    z = _log_p(best_p.reshape(B, l).sum(axis=1))
                    q = _softmax(z)
                    m = np.zeros(B, dtype=np.float64)
                    v = np.zeros(B, dtype=np.float64)
                    t = 0
                    lr_cur *= 0.78
                    hard_drop = max(0.0, hard_drop - 0.002)
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    lr_cur = max(0.005, lr_cur * 0.93)
                    no_improve = 0

            w = _lse_weights(h, beta)
            gp = _grad_h(p, w)
            gp = np.where(np.isfinite(gp), gp, 0.0)

            # dF/dq_j = (1/l) * sum_{i in block j} dF/dp_i
            gq = gp.reshape(B, l).sum(axis=1) / float(l)
            gq = np.where(np.isfinite(gq), gq, 0.0)

            gz = q * (gq - np.mean(gq))
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(gz)):
                break

            t += 1
            bias1 = 1.0 - b1 ** t
            bias2 = 1.0 - b2 ** t
            m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * gz
            v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (gz * gz)
            mhat = m / bias1
            vhat = v / bias2
            step = mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-8)
            step = np.clip(step, -float(step_clip), float(step_clip))
            z_new = z - float(lr_cur) * step
            z_new = np.where(np.isfinite(z_new), z_new, 0.0)

            q_new = _softmax(z_new)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(q_new)):
                z_new = _log_p(q)
                q_new = q

            z = z_new
            q = q_new

    return best_p, best_sc


def _active_polish(p_init, deadline,
                   tol_start=0.25, tol_end=1e-6,
                   lr_init=0.20, momentum=0.85,
                   max_line=18, min_time=2.0):
    """
    Active-peak gradient polish with momentum and backtracking line search.
    """
    p = _normalize(p_init)
    n = len(p)
    if n <= 0:
        return p, np.inf
    best = p.copy()
    best_sc = _score_norm(best)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    tol_frac = float(tol_start)
    lr = float(lr_init)
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - max(0.5, min_time):
        h = _convolve_self(p)
        hmax = float(h.max())
        sc = 2.0 * n * hmax
        if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc <= 0.0:
            break

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best = p.copy()
            best_sc = float(sc)
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        tol_abs = max(tol_frac * hmax, 1e-14)
        w = _active_weights(h, tol_abs)
        grad = _grad_h(p, w)
        grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm <= 1e-18:
            break

        v = float(momentum) * v - float(lr) * grad
        v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)

        alpha = 1.0
        improved = False
        current_sc = float(sc)

        for _ in range(int(max_line)):
            p_try = _normalize(p + alpha * v)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(p_try)):
                alpha *= 0.5
                continue
            h_try = _convolve_self(p_try)
            sc_try = 2.0 * n * float(h_try.max())
            if not np.isfinite(sc_try):
                alpha *= 0.5
                continue
            if sc_try < current_sc - 1e-15:
                p = p_try
                improved = True
                break
            alpha *= 0.5

        if not improved:
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            if no_improve % 15 == 0:
                tol_frac = max(float(tol_end), tol_frac * 0.62)
                lr = max(0.003, lr * 0.72)
            if no_improve > 350:
                p = best.copy()
                v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                tol_frac = max(float(tol_end), tol_frac * 0.45)
                lr = max(0.005, float(lr_init) * 0.55)
                no_improve = 0
        else:
            tol_frac = max(float(tol_end), tol_frac * 0.985)
            lr = min(float(lr_init) * 2.5, lr * 1.008)

    return best, best_sc


def _perturb_global(p, sigma, rng):
    z = _log_p(p)
    z = z + float(sigma) * rng.standard_normal(len(p))
    return _softmax(z)


def _perturb_block(p, sigma, rng):
    n = len(p)
    blen = max(16, n // 16)
    start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - blen)))
    z = _log_p(p)
    z[start:start + blen] += float(sigma) * rng.standard_normal(blen)
    return _softmax(z)


def _perturb_sparse(p, sigma, rng):
    n = len(p)
    k = max(16, n // 10)
    idx = rng.choice(n, size=k, replace=False)
    p2 = p.copy()
    factors = np.exp(float(sigma) * rng.standard_normal(k))
    p2[idx] *= factors
    return _normalize(p2)


def _perturb_mirror(p, sigma, rng):
    n = len(p)
    seg = max(16, n // 16)
    start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - seg)))
    p2 = p.copy()
    p2[start:start + seg] = p2[start:start + seg][::-1]
    z = _log_p(p2)
    z += float(sigma) * 0.15 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    return _softmax(z)


def _quick_polish(p, deadline, min_time=1.5):
    if time.monotonic() > deadline - max(1.0, min_time):
        return _normalize(p)
    p_out, _ = _active_polish(
        p, deadline,
        tol_start=0.35, tol_end=1e-5,
        lr_init=0.18, momentum=0.85,
        max_line=10, min_time=1.0
    )
    return _normalize(p_out)


def _ils_search(p_best, deadline, rng, min_time=60.0):
    """
    Stochastic iterated local search with diverse perturbations.
    """
    current = _normalize(p_best)
    current_sc = _score_norm(current)
    best = current.copy()
    best_sc = current_sc

    strategies = [
        ("global_small", 0.04),
        ("global_med", 0.12),
        ("global_large", 0.30),
        ("block", 0.20),
        ("block_large", 0.50),
        ("sparse", 0.25),
        ("sparse_large", 0.60),
        ("mirror", 0.08),
        ("global_very_small", 0.015),
        ("block_small", 0.08),
    ]
    strategies = [
        ("global", 0.03),
        ("global", 0.10),
        ("global", 0.25),
        ("block", 0.12),
        ("block", 0.35),
        ("sparse", 0.18),
        ("sparse", 0.55),
        ("mirror", 0.06),
        ("global", 0.012),
        ("block", 0.05),
    ]

    attempt = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - max(10.0, min_time):
        kind, sigma = strategies[attempt % len(strategies)]
        if kind == "global":
            p2 = _perturb_global(current, sigma, rng)
        elif kind == "block":
            p2 = _perturb_block(current, sigma, rng)
        elif kind == "sparse":
            p2 = _perturb_sparse(current, sigma, rng)
        elif kind == "mirror":
            p2 = _perturb_mirror(current, sigma, rng)
        else:
            p2 = current.copy()

        quick_deadline = min(
            deadline - max(10.0, min_time),
            time.monotonic() + 4.0
        )
        p_opt = _quick_polish(p2, quick_deadline, min_time=1.0)
        sc_opt = _score_norm(p_opt)

        if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < best_sc - 1e-13:
            best = p_opt.copy()
            best_sc = float(sc_opt)
            current = p_opt.copy()
            current_sc = float(sc_opt)
            t = time.monotonic() - PROPOSE_START
            print(f"    ILS new best: {best_sc:.10f} ({kind}, sig={sigma:.3g}, t={t:.1f}s)")
        elif np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < current_sc - 1e-13:
            current = p_opt.copy()
            current_sc = float(sc_opt)

        attempt += 1

    return best, best_sc


def _block_search(p_base, n, B, deadline, rng):
    """
    Try several block-constant profiles and return best expanded sequence.
    """
    n = int(n)
    B = int(B)
    if B <= 0 or n <= 0 or n % B != 0:
        return _normalize(p_base), _score_norm(_normalize(p_base))

    l = n // B
    best = _normalize(p_base)
    best_sc = _score_norm(best)

    starts = []

    # Base profile lumped into blocks.
    if len(p_base) == n:
        pb = np.asarray(p_base, dtype=np.float64).reshape(B, l)
        starts.append(_normalize(pb.sum(axis=1)))

    # Uniform.
    starts.append(np.ones(B, dtype=np.float64))

    # Low-frequency profiles in log-space.
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, B, dtype=np.float64)
    starts.append(_softmax(0.70 * (2.0 * t - 1.0)))                 # rising
    starts.append(_softmax(0.70 * (1.0 - 2.0 * t)))                 # falling
    starts.append(_softmax(0.90 * np.cos(np.pi * t)))               # U-shaped
    starts.append(_softmax(-0.90 * np.cos(np.pi * t)))              # center peak
    starts.append(_softmax(1.10 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t)))         # one wave
    starts.append(_softmax(1.30 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * t)))         # two waves
    starts.append(_softmax(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, B)))                # random

    # Remove near duplicates by simple checksum.
    uniq = []
    seen = set()
    for q in starts:
        q = _normalize(q)
        key = tuple(np.round(q, 8).astype(np.int64).tolist())
        if key not in seen:
            seen.add(key)
            uniq.append(q)

    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - now - 5.0)
    if remaining <= 0.0:
        return best, best_sc

    per = max(12.0, remaining / float(max(1, len(uniq))))

    for i, q0 in enumerate(uniq):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
            break
        phase_deadline = min(deadline - 8.0, time.monotonic() + per)
        p_candidate, sc_candidate = _adam_block(
            q0, n, B, phase_deadline,
            lr=0.09, b1=0.90, b2=0.999,
            step_clip=1.0, min_time=3.0
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc_candidate) and sc_candidate < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best = p_candidate.copy()
            best_sc = float(sc_candidate)
            t = time.monotonic() - PROPOSE_START
            print(f"    Block candidate {i} improved: {best_sc:.10f} (t={t:.1f}s)")

    return best, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    """
    Main search entry point.  Returns a list of non-negative floats.
    """
    global PROPOSE_START

    PROPOSE_START = time.monotonic()
    start = PROPOSE_START
    deadline = start + max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 15.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**63 - 1))

    best = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    # Load parent if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        parent_arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if parent_arr.size > 0:
            parent = _normalize(parent_arr)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    n_main = 24576

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        if parent.size == n_main:
            best = parent.copy()
        else:
            best = _resample(parent, n_main)
    else:
        best = _normalize(np.ones(n_main, dtype=np.float64))

    best_sc = _score_norm(best)
    print(f"Initial: n={best.size}, score={best_sc:.10f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 1: direct active polish from the parent/base.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 300.0:
        dl = min(deadline - 250.0, start + 260.0)
        p, sc = _active_polish(
            best, dl,
            tol_start=0.30, tol_end=1e-6,
            lr_init=0.22, momentum=0.85,
            max_line=18, min_time=5.0
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best = p.copy()
            best_sc = float(sc)
            print(f"Phase1 active polish: {best_sc:.10f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 2: smoothed log-sum-exp search.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 220.0:
        dl = min(deadline - 180.0, time.monotonic() + 170.0)
        p, sc = _adam_full(best, dl, lr=0.025, step_clip=0.35)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best = p.copy()
            best_sc = float(sc)
            print(f"Phase2 ADAM full: {best_sc:.10f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 3: block-reduced search for a new basin.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0:
        dl = min(deadline - 140.0, time.monotonic() + 230.0)
        p, sc = _block_search(best, n_main, 256, dl, rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best = p.copy()
            best_sc = float(sc)
            print(f"Phase3 block 256: {best_sc:.10f}")
        else:
            # A coarser block size can sometimes find a different basin.
            if time.monotonic() < dl - 20.0:
                p2, sc2 = _block_search(p, n_main, 128, dl, rng)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                    best = p2.copy()
                    best_sc = float(sc2)
                    print(f"Phase3 block 128: {best_sc:.10f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 4: another active polish after block/ADAM moves.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 160.0:
        dl = min(deadline - 130.0, time.monotonic() + 210.0)
        p, sc = _active_polish(
            best, dl,
            tol_start=0.18, tol_end=1e-7,
            lr_init=0.16, momentum=0.84,
            max_line=18, min_time=5.0
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best = p.copy()
            best_sc = float(sc)
            print(f"Phase4 active polish: {best_sc:.10f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 5: stochastic iterated local search.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        dl = min(deadline - 90.0, time.monotonic() + 330.0)
        p, sc = _ils_search(best, dl, rng, min_time=70.0)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best = p.copy()
            best_sc = float(sc)
            print(f"Phase5 ILS: {best_sc:.10f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 6: try a longer sequence.  32768 is usually still fast enough
    # and can reduce finite-length effects.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0:
        n2 = 32768
        p2 = _resample(best, n2)
        sc2 = _score_norm(p2)
        dl = min(deadline - 60.0, time.monotonic() + 230.0)
        p2, sc2 = _active_polish(
            p2, dl,
            tol_start=0.22, tol_end=1e-7,
            lr_init=0.18, momentum=0.85,
            max_line=18, min_time=5.0
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
            best = p2.copy()
            best_sc = float(sc2)
            print(f"Phase6 longer n={n2}: {best_sc:.10f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 7: final fine polish.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
        dl = deadline - 2.0
        p, sc = _active_polish(
            best, dl,
            tol_start=0.08, tol_end=1e-8,
            lr_init=0.10, momentum=0.83,
            max_line=16, min_time=2.0
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best = p.copy()
            best_sc = float(sc)
            print(f"Phase7 final polish: {best_sc:.10f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Sanitize and return.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    out = np.asarray(best, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)

    s = float(out.sum())
    if s < 0.01 or not np.isfinite(s):
        out = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(out.sum())

    # Scale to a comfortable magnitude.  Scale invariance makes this safe.
    out = out * (1000.0 / s)

    # Respect evaluator's upper cap.
    mx = float(out.max())
    if mx > 1000.0:
        out = out * (1000.0 / mx)

    # A final internal score report.
    out_norm = _normalize(out)
    final_sc = _score_norm(out_norm)
    print("-" * 60)
    print(f"FINAL internal score: {final_sc:.10f}, n={out.size}, "
          f"time={time.monotonic() - PROPOSE_START:.2f}s")

    try:
        ext = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in out])
        print(f"FINAL external evaluate_sequence: {ext}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
